## 📐 Rulings of Record (register; narrative in Session Log archives)

**Import/identity (July 14–15):** IMP-1..8 + amendments 1–4 (dictionary identity: HistoryID primary / row-hash fallback / `assigned_history_id` minted; mirror re-runs w/ mandatory dry-run + auto-snapshot; no DB staging; staged table scope; provenance tables only; spec-of-record discipline; Category Start Number → rank-based version-sort; Area = IGNORE(computed); **method '0' is LOAD-BEARING**) · IMP-CD text-epoch-dates variance-and-drop permanent · 2B-1 upgrade-in-place · **2B-DUP N-to-N stable pairing** (hash contract FROZEN in spec.ts) · 2C-1..4 · (jj)-OQ1 tie order `created_at DESC, id DESC` (dissolved below group by the sort spec; stands as the stable underlay) · (jj)-OQ2 usage = MODEL match · (jj)-OQ3 throw-loud fetches.

**Export (July 15 PM; unit deferred but rulings stand):** EXP-0..7 · RC-1..10 (**HistoryID emits UPPERCASE**) · D-EXP-3 exact numerics · type='generic' export scope. **S-12 rider (July 19): at revival, re-align export order with the ruled chains** (see Build Order item 2).

**Category-fill on model change (July 20 PM; CAT-1..CAT-4, all Daniel-confirmed — matches v1 D1 step 2, "fill only if blank"):** CAT-1 category is NEVER overwritten on a model change — a record that already carries a category keeps it (v1 functional truth; the "coverage extension" is a per-record blank-fill, not an overwrite). CAT-2 the fill is SILENT — no prompt (both the edited record's own fill and the sibling blank-fill). CAT-3 on a "Change All" commit the new model's default category fills every AFFECTED sibling whose category is blank, and only those; "Just this one" writes no siblings (the edited record still gets its own blank-fill). CAT-4 no new dialog. **Architecture of record:** the new-model default category (`newCat`) is resolved ONCE from the library, independent of the edited record's blankness; the sibling blank set is read fresh (`equipment_items.category_id` for the sibling ids) and filled via a SEPARATE scoped `propagateFieldToIds('category_id', …)` — category no longer rides the unconditional model+note sibling write. Guardrail candidate: a future cascade pass must not re-fold category into the model+note sibling write (that was the clobber-or-skip bug).

**Model-band key is category-aware (July 20 PM):** a model band's identity is the natural triple **`outerLabel␟category␟model`** (U+001F separators), produced by ONE shared pure helper `modelBandKey(outerLabel, catLabel, model, fallbackId)` and used at BOTH derivation sites (the render closure's `groupKey` and `resolveModelGroupKeyFor`) so nav-derived and render-derived keys never diverge. Blank model → falls back to the first record's id (unchanged — preserves `wasPending`/`newRowId` new-row anchor semantics). The three `data-model-group` `querySelector` consumers escape the key with `CSS.escape()`. Guardrail candidate: never derive a model-band key from model text alone — it collides when one model straddles two category (or outer-group) bands, which is now normal under CAT-1.

**Focus-follow on existing-record model commit (July 21 AM; FF-1..FF-5; punch (rr) closed; commit `c23f92d`):**
- **FF-1** plain commit (blur / outside-click / unmount net) on a CHANGED model → re-open the SAME record's model band editor at its post-resort position, text selected, center-scrolled — **orphan-guarded:** the reclaim fires only if `document.activeElement` fell to body/documentElement; a click that landed on a real interactive target wins (no focus steal, no scroll yank).
- **FF-2** typed Enter → open the model band editor of the record BELOW in the **POST-sort** order (supersedes the pre-sort synchronous advance); end-of-list clamps to the edited record itself. Mechanically retires the fragile single-rAF locator on this path — the 5-retry `pendingBandFocusRef` locator is the mechanism.
- **FF-3** Tab / arrow-highlight+Enter / dropdown mouse-pick → Quantity of the SAME record ("next field"), selected, center-scrolled — semantics unchanged from slot 2. **Amendment of record:** the landing is DEFERRED past cascade dialogs when a changed-model cascade is in flight (immediate landing collided with Change All / note prompts stealing focus after it); pristine path keeps the immediate `landInQuantity`. No orphan guard on this arm (keyboard gesture — land unconditionally).
- **FF-4** pristine or blank commits: NO follow, any gesture (nothing moved; nothing to chase). This is FF-1's loop-breaker — the reclaim happens once per actual change; a second click-away commits pristine and the editor stays closed. Enter on a pristine band keeps today's immediate band advance.
- **FF-5 (architecture of record):** the follow rides the cascade `finally` as an added existing-record branch AFTER `releasePin()` and the untouched `wasPending` new-record line — S-7 ordering extended, never reordered. Gesture identity reaches the cascade via `modelCascadeInFlightRef` + `modelCommitGestureRef` set by the band editor's advance callbacks (which fire synchronously AFTER `commitValue→onCommit` — recon-proven at every gesture) — **`AutocompleteInput`'s commit contract is unchanged and the component stays byte-untouched.** Landing resolution happens post-settle in a `pendingBandFollow` effect against fresh `grouped`/`orderedIds` (never the handler's stale closure), through `resolveModelGroupKeyFor` → the triple band key. `ConfirmDialog` verified safe: focuses on open only, restores nothing on close — the deferred landing is the last focus action and wins.

**(kk) bridge build rulings (KK-0..KK-8, July 21; all Daniel-confirmed — "all approved"):**
- **KK-0** four gated legs A (core wiring + read-mostly pages) → B (equipment list) → C ((ss)) → D (images, Trigger B at open). **Storage-cleanup is not separately buildable** — recon proved zero storage existed, so it folded into leg D as the IMG-4 rider.
- **KK-1** architecture of record: pages convert from server-component fetch to thin server shells rendering `'use client'` loaders that run `useQuery` per read (browser supabase client + unchanged `fetchAllRows`; identical RLS/session — security-neutral; layout server auth gates stay). First visit fetches as before; every later visit paints instantly from cache with silent background revalidate. (Next-native-prefetch-only alternative presented and rejected: no freshness story, speculative full-fetch per hover.)
- **KK-2** `src/lib/queryKeys.ts` is the ONE key source (pure, tested). Family prefix `['project', projectId]` = the invalidation handle. Shared reads share keys — `v_equipment_list` per project is ONE cache entry read by equipment, print, and revisions.
- **KK-3** invalidation doctrine: on converted pages `router.refresh()`-as-data-refresh retires → scoped `invalidateQueries`; `router.refresh()` is retained ONLY where server-rendered chrome depends on it (settings save → layout project name/color). Cross-page writes invalidate by family. Call sites classified by recon table, never pattern-replaced.
- **KK-4** equipment-list contract: the `initialItems` seam and resync effect are PRESERVED (query data feeds the same prop). (a) **Unmount write-back** — leaving the list snapshots `itemsRef.current` into the cache so returning paints exactly what was left, never a stale flash. (b) **Guarded resync** — while any in-flight signal is active (`editingModelGroupKey`, cascade in flight, open dialog, pending new record) an incoming resync is STASHED and applied when signals clear; **latest-stash-wins** (CC OQ-2 disposition — newest server truth applied once, no merge/conflict resolver). Transient sub-tiebreak reorder on write-back paint accepted (CC OQ-3 — self-heals on the immediate refetch; mirrors the standing (jj) tie-order caveat).
- **KK-5** preload: project-menu mount prefetches the library reads (leg A) + equipment reads (leg B). Print/revisions inherit equipment's warm cache through shared keys.
- **KK-6** declared visual outcome per leg: 16/16 at 0.0000%, any diff = stop-and-report (held at every checkpoint of every pass). First-visit loading state = existing chrome, render-null while loading.
- **KK-7** the vestigial `?noMethod=1` becomes a real consumer via (ss)'s param machinery: Show Me → equipment list with method filter FILTER_NONE. `landModel` wins if both params present.
- **KK-8** DB surface: zero for legs A–C (ruled at open and held); leg D = the Trigger B surface (bucket + policies migration, Arch-applied + smoked).

**(ss) build rulings (SS-Q1 + SS-1..SS-3, July 21, leg C; spec locked July 20 — both directions, model field selected+focused):**
- **SS-Q1 = A** — context-aware nav chords: ⌥⇧W on the list with a focused row lands on that model in the library; ⌥⇧L on the library with a focused row lands on the matching list record (model band editor opened). No focused row / blank model / no match → plain navigation, silently. Zite has NO matching-jump precedent (header pills are plain nav) — this is a v2 extension, chord-only, zero new chrome.
- **SS-1** match = exact model text, then case-insensitive fallback. **SS-2** multiple matches → first in current DISPLAY order (list: grouped/orderedIds; library: `sorted`). **SS-3** the `?land=` param is consumed ONCE and scrubbed via `history.replaceState` — refresh/bookmark never re-lands; the scrub PRESERVES `window.history.state` (A-rider hardening — never null it).
- **Mechanism of record:** capture-phase window keydown listeners in each client claim their one chord ahead of `useNavShortcuts`'s bubble listener (`preventDefault` + `stopImmediatePropagation`; all other chords flow through; the shared hook is byte-untouched). List chord runs the K-5 flush before navigating; library chord blurs (its writes are fire-and-forget, matching existing edit-then-navigate behavior — CC OQ-2 accepted). `landTarget.ts` (`findLandTarget`) is the ONE matcher for both sides. Landing effects: list → editing-key + `pendingBandFocusRef` (FF idiom, no new locator); library → `data-lib-row-id` + local 5-retry rAF locator → model field focus+select, center-scrolled. Post-landing the open band editor is a KK-4b in-flight signal, so the first resync stashes until it closes — benign, on record.

**Project images build rulings (IMG-1..IMG-6 + A1..A5, July 21, leg D; product rulings (a) scope = the three settings zones (b) PNG/JPEG ≤5MB (c) replace-in-place, no version history):**
- **IMG-1** ONE private bucket `project-images`; server-enforced 5,242,880-byte cap + `image/png`/`image/jpeg` allowlist; object path `{projectId}/{slot}` (slot = `top_left` · `top_right` · `box_logo`), contentType set on upload.
- **IMG-2** four storage policies (select/insert/update/delete, authenticated) mirror the house ownership pattern: allowed iff the path's first folder is a project with `owner_id = auth.uid()`. One security doctrine, applied to storage. Migration `storage_project_images_bucket_and_policies` (Arch-applied via MCP; smoked read-only: bucket + all four policies verified).
- **IMG-3** replace-in-place: fixed path per slot + `upsert: true` — no versions, no orphans on replace. **Columns store the storage PATH**; display resolves via short-lived signed URLs at mount. Legacy branch of record: a stored value starting with `http` renders directly and ✕ nulls the column only.
- **IMG-4** delete-project cleanup: `removeAllProjectImages` (list folder + remove; NEVER throws) — **ordered BEFORE the row delete (A1, ruling of record): the policies verify ownership against the live projects row, so a post-delete remove is denied and orphans everything.** Best-effort: the delete proceeds regardless of cleanup `{ok}`.
- **IMG-5** print boundary: recon found `top_right_image_url` is print's only image consumer (rendered directly as src). **Fold-in ruled (A3):** print resolves a non-http path to a signed URL in its data mapping (cancelled-guarded effect; unresolved renders no image, exactly as before). Top-left render → registered onto the Print header line2 + autosize unit; box-logo render → Sprint-4 box labels. Print needs no cache-buster (per-mount resolve — CC verdict accepted).
- **IMG-6** sequencing held: migration drafted → Daniel confirmed → Arch applied + smoked → CC client pass.
- **A2** image-column writes are DIRECT single-column updates + `projectSingle` invalidation — never the shared debounced autoSave (a superseded debounce silently dropped the column write while the storage upload succeeded).
- **A4** drag-and-drop through the same validate→upload path as the picker (the zone's own label promised it); no at-rest render change (frame 06 byte-identical).
- **A5** replace re-resolve: root = the resolve effect keyed on the (stable) path never re-fired on a same-path replace. Fix = `resolveNonce` bumped per successful upload (effect dep) + a display-only `&cb=` cache-buster on the signed URL (the stored value stays the bare path). ✕-remove needs no equivalent (null is a real dep change).

Commit fires, advance is immediate, async patch resolution interrupts from the new focus.**

**Sort spec (July 17, supersedes (qq)) — IMPLEMENTED July 18–19 (sort unit):** by category: category→model→method→description→group · by method: method→category→model→description→group · by box: box→category→model→method→description→group. After group: stable internal tiebreak, engineering hygiene only. Chains apply to SCREEN and PRINT identically; all model comparisons use `compareModels` (SRT-D1..D4).

**Sort-unit build rulings (S-1..S-12, July 18–19; all Daniel-confirmed):**
- **S-1** chain module `src/lib/equipment/sortChains.ts` — `makeEquipmentChainComparator(mode, methodRankByName)`; category term = `category_sort` rank nulls-last then label; method term = `sort_order` rank nulls-last then label; model = `compareModels`; box/description/group = `compareLabels`; returns 0 below the last term (JS stable sort ⇒ the Layer-1 server chain persists as the don't-care tiebreak; server chain itself untouched). Screen sorts `filtered` before grouping. **(kk)-era note (July 21): the Layer-1 underlay sort now applies inside the equipment query fn — one place, verbatim semantics.**

**FLT-batch build rulings (D-1..D-8, July 19; all Daniel-confirmed):**
- **D-1** filter-bar "Display All (⌥⇧J)" button renders ONLY when a column filter is active; fourth sibling after the Group dropdown; renders nothing at rest.
- **D-2** FLT-1 precedence, per field: concrete filter beats the New Defaults bar (name-resolved, null on no-match — never invented); FILTER_NONE seeds null overriding a set default; FILTER_HAS / FILTER_ANY fall through; `remaining_quantity` never filter-affected. **July-23 extension: the cable surface mirrors D-2 for Group/Method AND Bundle (bundle-filter-born membership); the cable header filters are free-text autocompletes with NO sentinels — the sentinel clause reduces to concrete/empty.**
- **D-3** inheritance fires on filter state alone — find activity neither enables nor suppresses it.
- **D-4** `showAll` = `displayAll` + clear three filters, a NEW wrapper — never folded into `displayAll`. Consumers: ⌥⇧J (find-or-filter gate), FOUND-strip Display All, the D-1 button. Wire: `onDisplayAll={findActive ? showAll : displayAll}`, FindPanel byte-untouched.
- **D-5** exemption widening: `createNewItem` AND `duplicateItem` grant the visibility exemption on find-or-filter-active; any filter change clears `exemptNewIds`; duplicate = verbatim copy; Library "+ Add" untouched. **July-24 note: cable's 680 post-write does NOT use the exemption (CP-B4 moves the filters instead — see the cable rulings below).**
- **D-6** seeding logic extracted to pure `buildNewItemSeed` (`newItemSeed.ts`).
- **D-7** defaults-override affordance: Method/Group default fields dim opacity-50 + native title when `isDefaultOverridden(filterX)`; dim only, never disabled; Mark All As Remaining never dims.
- **D-8** filter-pulldown type-ahead = punch (iii), rides Sprint 4.

**Split-unit build rulings (SP-1..SP-9 + SP-1a/SP-7a, July 20; all Daniel-confirmed; standing detail in CLAUDE.md "Split architecture of record"):**
- **SP-1** one Zite-styled `SplitDialog` — **amended by SP-1a** for the over-split path.
- **SP-1a** over-split (n ≥ Q) is a SEPARATE second dialog — `OverSplitDialog`, v1 prompt shape, Zite copy verbatim, `brand-600` accent; its Cancel/Escape aborts entirely. NOT an in-place OK→"Add Instead" flip.
- **SP-2** gate: row glyph + ⌥⇧S enabled on `quantity > 1`; type guard (`generic` only) enforced in the pure module.
- **SP-3** split glyph on the current row only, absolutely-positioned leading-edge overlay, zero layout shift, one `useStableCallback` id-as-argument.
- **SP-4** new-row payload = `duplicateItem` copy shape + dialog overrides; exemption under find-or-filter; NO anchor pin.
- **SP-5** remaining = v1 proportional, CONSERVED: `newRem = min(n, max(0, round(R·n/Q)))`, source = R − newRem; null stays null. Add-instead: source untouched, new remaining = `markAllRemaining ? n : null`.
- **SP-6** write order = insert-first then decrement; decrement via the `propagateFieldToIds` idiom (NOT `trackedUpdateItem`); on decrement failure the clone is deleted (compensation). **Observable-programmatic-write pattern of record.**
- **SP-7/7a** post-split AND post-add focus lands in DESCRIPTION, selected, centered — via the K-2 seam.
- **SP-8** ⌥⇧S = a `KeyS` case in `useNavShortcuts` dispatching a per-route `onSplit` target.
- **SP-9** `SplitDialog` on the ConfirmDialog idiom; `splitTarget` stage machine; both confirm paths run one `executeSplit` primitive.

- **DL-1..7 (July 23, DL-6 amended twice; final state = WF-1, July 24):** delegation rearchitecture — unit-scale handoffs, Parity Rule (uncited = unbuilt; CC bounce license), evidence judged at CC's desk, response-quality standard, throwaway-smoke standing. **WF-1 (Daniel ruling, July 24) SUPERSEDES the DL-6 commit-execution grant: CC NEVER stages, commits, or pushes — every unit ends at green gates + the handoff file, and Arch delivers Daniel ONE exact pastable terminal block per commit point (`cd` · stage by explicit filename · `git status --short` verify with the expected staged set spelled out · `git commit` · `git push`). The July-23 per-commit CC-execution authority is RESCINDED; push remains human-only (Strat invariant 1). Enforcement rides `.claude/settings.json` (commit joins push in the deny set).** Canonical text: Arch Prompt v27 + the CC Operating Contract inside CLAUDE.md.
- **WF-2 (Daniel-confirmed, July 24) — per-unit CC model tier:** every handoff carries a `Tier:` line (Arch's call per unit; Daniel overrides at will). **Opus = the default** for fully-ruled builds, recon passes, test-writing, mechanical follow-ups. **Fable = by exception** for units judging ambiguous evidence at CC's desk, frozen-adjacent work, or where OPEN QUESTIONS quality is the product. Escalation tripwire: weak OQs, evidence misreads, or a missed halt on an Opus run → that unit's remainder returns to Fable (+ a Corrections entry if the tier call was Arch's). No mid-unit tier switches — changes land at unit boundaries. Datapoints on record: CTF (held) · B4 (one invention defect, weighed, no escalation) · R7 + S3-F (held; S3-F's FIX-1/FIX-2 root-cause work incl. falsifying two Arch hypotheses with evidence — the tier performing above its brief). **July-29 addition (S3-F): mid-unit SESSION handovers on CC's own context-budget flag are licensed and encouraged** — CC stopping at a checkpoint boundary rather than opening the hardest checkpoint on a thinning budget is the correct call; sessions are disposable, CLAUDE.md + the handoff files are the durable memory; the resume paste declares the expected dirty set so a fresh session doesn't misread mid-unit work as drift.

- **(ooo)-2 / O2-1..4 (July 24, Daniel-confirmed; BUILT `6a7e297`) — the library sort of record, superseding the first (ooo) fence:** **O2-1** categories order numerically as a human reads them (1.0 < 9.0 < 11.0 < 13.0 < 14.5) via the equipment list's OWN ordering source — `categories.sort_order` rank ASC nulls-last, then `compareLabels` on the name (one truth; rank is the PRIMARY term — if a rank ever disagrees with the numeric name, the library follows the equipment list, documented in a test). **O2-2** models within a category use `compareModels` (imported unmodified; byte-agreement proven on Daniel's capture vector). **O2-3** the sort key IS the displayed value: stored category text resolves by the select's own exact-match doctrine; no-match/empty → rank +∞ → the trailing uncategorized block — a row rendering "—" sorts uncategorized regardless of stale text underneath. **O2-4** `librarySort.ts` (`makeLibraryComparator(categories)` factory, useMemo-keyed on the live category list) is THE library comparator. **Defect mechanism of record:** the page sorted on raw stored text while displaying the resolved select value — library categories are text references (not FKs), so deletes/renames orphan text; orphaned rows rendered "—" but sorted mid-list under invisible text, and the plain localeCompare put "11.0" before "9.0". Scope fence held: `sortChains`/`cableSortChains`/`modelSort` and every equipment/cable consumer byte-untouched (imports only).
- **CTF unit rulings (July 24; BUILT `c1d7ae6` — CT-1..4 ruled July 24 AM + the session's amendments; the dialog is REUSABLE — S3-F wired edit mode July 29–31, then D3-AMEND-2 superseded row-click-edit with the inline grid; the dialog remains the CREATE path):**
  - **CT-1..4 (spec):** v1's nine-modal cable-type cascade (evidence ctf_01..09, COMMITTED) consolidates to **ONE dialog, four sections**: ①Ends & Lines (source/dest end, mult line count, auto-add-lines ☐ → `add_lines`) ②Labels (**Big ☐/Small ☐ → `default_label_size` big/small/both/none** via a pure tested mapping fn; Branch Cable Type autocomplete) ③Specifications (OD in / weight lbs-ft / reference) ④Tails (auto-add ☐, source/dest tail models, tail method, five tail label COUNTS numeric — **CT-3: collected now, behavior INERT until S3-D**). **CT-1 trigger:** unmatched Model commit (both prior-state branches — create-seed new record and existing-record edit converge on `handleCommitModel`, dispatched via pure `classifyModelCommit`) opens "New Cable Type: name"; Save creates + the triggering commit resolves; Cancel creates nothing + reverts (existing: no-op revert per the B2 pattern; new: back to prior/empty). Blank explicit commit still clears a set type (intent, kept). **The interim silent clear is RETIRED — no path silently clears.** All fields optional; Save-with-blanks = v1's bare-type path. Structural divergences ruled deliberate: wizard→single dialog (CT-1); v1's numeric pairs→checkboxes (CT-4; schema stores only the enum).
  - **R1 (migration `ctf_widen_default_label_size_check`, Arch-applied + smoked July 24):** the live CHECK admitted only big/small — widened to the four values; existing rows untouched; NULL legal; invalid values proven refused; authenticated grants proven; smoke rolled back clean. *(Generated TS types carry no CHECK domains — regen produced zero diff, expected.)*
  - **CTF-G1 (as amended) — the creation queue, BRANCH ONLY:** unrecognized names never stack dialogs mid-flight — queued FIFO, opened after the current Save, recursively; parent Cancel kills its queue; dequeue-resolve guard (a queued name that now resolves never re-creates — and a resolving BRANCH back-patches to the existing id). **R2 (FK arm):** parent Save stores `branch_cable_type_id` NULL on unresolved; the queued Save BACK-PATCHES the saved parent (loud save-error on failure, never silent); queued Cancel leaves the NULL. **F4+F8 SPEC CORRECTION (Daniel gate): tails are NOT cable types** — source/destination tail models are PLAIN TEXT fields of the cable_types record (the columns' actual shape); typing a tail name never creates a type and never queues; the tail arm was REMOVED from the queue. `UNIQUE (project_id, model_name)` = the DB backstop; duplicate-name creation surfaces a readable error, dialog kept open.
  - **R3 + F3 (prefills, per ctf_03/ctf_08 v1 functional truth + Daniel riders):** dialog opens Small ☑ (untouched Save writes 'small'), Sets of Line Labels = 1, **Small Tail Block Labels = 1**, **smart mult-line count** (`parseSmartMultLineCount`: a leading integer + pair/pr/ch/channel token in the NAME — "19-Pair"/"19pr"/"19ch" — seeds Number of Mult Lines; word-boundary guarded; no match → blank), tail-model placeholders "e.g., 19-Pair G3M-XLRF Tails, NL4 AB-Y" (source) / "…G3F-XLRM…" (destination); all else blank.
  - **F5–F7, F9 (usability, Daniel gate):** Tab traverses every field including OUT of an empty Tail Method (optional; empty commits nothing; `onAdvance` + capture-phase shift ref) · Tail Method hint "(if blank, new tails use the project's default method)" — **REGISTERED S3-D RIDER: honored at S3-D** · sticky dialog header · **Tail Method = autocomplete text input (the Sprint-3 constraint enforced; a methods FK, set-existing-only, null on no-match — correctly NOT in the queue; blur-commit/Save race guarded by a synchronous ref mirror)** · F6: the row Model field verified already the v1-parity type-to-narrow autocomplete (no change needed) · F7: Description = fixed v1-proportion width (176px, CC's delegated judgment) + trailing flex-1 spacer BOTH header and row — surplus collects FAR RIGHT; wide windows scroll-free; the <~1508px intrinsic residual joins (kkk) · F9: the dialog's Tail Method strips the inherited `--equip-sel-input-bg` project tint → neutral input styling (dialog context only).
  - **F10 (model display, root-caused):** the Model cell bound to the phantom `v_cable_list.model` (a real column, EMPTY for cables) — fresh loads rendered typed cables blank; in-session edits masked it via optimistic writes. **Pre-existing latent at `6a7e297` (byte-proven), surfaced by CTF making typed cables reachable.** Fix: `cableModelDisplay(row)` = `cable_type_name ?? ''` — the view-truth field — used by the Model cell and the Change-All preview; regression-guarded (the phantom column is never read). Phantom writes retirement = the registered punch item.
  - **Accepted dispositions:** post-Save cascade onto the triggering row = MODEL ONLY (the other dialog fields are type-level metadata with no cable-row columns; auto-adds inert till S3-D) · field shows prior value behind the modal (cosmetic, hidden) · dequeue back-patch to a resolved existing id.
- **B3 unit rulings (July 25; BUILT `03b9753` — B3-R1/R2 ruled at open, R1a + A1–A7 from Daniel's gates; the dialog is REUSABLE, B4 wires the band-name trigger):**
  - **B3-R1** bundle creation lives on the "+" affordance ONLY; the row Bundle cell keeps B2 join-existing / no-op-revert byte-for-byte. **B3-R1a (A4):** while the "+" is visible, Enter/Tab in the Bundle cell ACTIVATE it (identical code path; Tab additionally advances — silent-create path only; the no-length path returns focus to the cell on Cancel). "+"-hidden commits keep B2 semantics untouched.
  - **B3-R2** ONE Update Bundle dialog, two modes: CREATE (the no-length "+" path, b3_01) + UPDATE (R-33, anatomy b2a_02; **triggered in B3 by clicking a BUNDLED cable's Length cell — v1 220; unbundled Length keeps inline editing byte-for-byte**). Band-name trigger + full update wire = **B4 riders**. Keyboard entry into a bundled Length cell: none exists (bundled rows skip Length in the ruled Tab order) — mouse-only stands, no Tab-order change.
  - **A1** the "+" renders only on a genuinely NEW name (non-empty AND no exact-existing match, findExactBundle doctrine, case-insensitive/trimmed); hidden on blank and on existing (join stays the autocomplete's job); the "Bundle" default-name path REMOVED.
  - **A3 (ruled v2 DEVIATION from v1 script 157):** the no-length create DEFERS THE INSERT TO SAVE — opening the dialog creates nothing; Cancel leaves NO bundle, NO join, typed text retained, "+" still visible; Save = create + join, patching effective faces from the INSERT data with no re-read (the clobber-fix doctrine — the B2 join-EXISTING path still re-reads, correctly); blank-length Save LEGAL (a length-less bundle — the registered S3-G print guard watches that state); a join failure compensates by deleting the orphan bundle (SP-6). The silent-create path (adoptable length) is UNCHANGED per 157: create + join immediately, no dialog.
  - **Membership at creation = the anchoring cable ONLY** (b3_01; bulk enrollment = Change All, Daniel ruling — no found-set membership built).
  - **A5** dark control chrome (cable flag checkboxes + steck radios rendering light-mode boxes in dark theme): PRE-EXISTING at `745e447` (B2-line), line-proven — `accent-color` never drove the native box chrome; fix = cable-scoped `color-scheme` keyed off the `.dark` scope (palette-publication rule; no literals; equipment controls untouched).
  - **A6 (dialog idiom of record):** select-on-entry (U2-1) in both modes — update focuses+selects length, create focuses+selects the carried name. Root cause: the persisted dialog instance reset the caret on reconcile; **remount-per-open via a keyed seq** (the NewCableTypeDialog precedent) is the dialog idiom of record.
  - **A7 (portal doctrine of record):** an OPEN autocomplete menu is ALWAYS topmost. Mechanism: opt-in `portalDropdown` on the shared AutocompleteInput — portal to body, fixed at the input rect, viewport-clamped, flips UPWARD when no room below (the last-row-above-the-inspector case); threaded to all nine cable-surface call sites; equipment callers take the unchanged absolute branch (byte-identical; 16/16 warm-run fence proof); the commit contract untouched. Clip root pre-existing (B2): the list wrapper's `overflow-x-auto` + the fixed bottom inspector. Box (empty registry until Sprint 4) + CTF Branch (native datalist, browser-topmost) covered by mechanism.

- **R7 unit rulings (July 28–29; BUILT `3acbee8` — R7-1..6 + OQ-R7-1..3 + R7-A1..A5; design gate first; fourteen scripts extracted; standing architecture in the CLAUDE.md R7 fold at `5b2a689`):**
  - **R7-1** the Make Patch? dialog KEPT v1-verbatim (668's anatomy) — no auto-resolve. **R7-A1 (amendment of record, Daniel's gate):** the default is the OPPOSITE end of the edited one (668 source-side defaults Destination; 678 destination-side defaults Source) — the build's uniform default was the OQ-R7-3 symmetry error, Arch-owned. **R7-A2:** origin-identifying dialog copy.

  - **R7-4 (FINAL on 686/687):** fresh/re-patch/steal + SILENT unpatch — victims' patched-end TEXT blanked; description/color untouched at unpatch; the INITIATOR never swept (ruled mechanism divergence, outcome parity — v1's token loop degenerates to pairwise under FKs). Delete-path partner `_end`+text sweep on BOTH delete paths (ON DELETE SET NULL only nulls the `_id`).
  - **R7-5** Change All routes per-occurrence through the same funnel (the B4 escape valve correctly not taken — no bulk-patch policy invented; **superseded in part by the S3-F FIX-1 sequencing rule for mixed batches**, see below).

Gated by the **Update Patches dialog** per 688 verbatim minus the dissolved-clones clause (S3E-R6 mechanical omission); **Cancel skips propagation only — the initiator's edit STANDS** (a ruled divergence from v1's Cancel Halt). **R7-A3b:** the device autocomplete corpus += effective-mult names (Daniel v1 attestation).
  - **R7-AMEND OQ#2 (Daniel ruling → the S3-F bulk rider, since BUILT):** a bulk op touching patched records raises ONE Update Patches dialog for the whole op, affected count in the copy; OK propagates for all; Cancel skips propagation only.
- **S3-F unit rulings (July 29–31; BUILT `327c100` — SR-1 + S3F-R1..R9 + R6-AMEND-1/2 + R3-AMEND + R7-AMEND-1 + D3-AMEND-1/2 + FIX-1/2/3 + W1–W3; design gate first; seven scripts extracted (609/665/421–424/681/683; 668/678 re-supplied); one mid-unit session handover on CC's context flag; three Daniel browser-gate rounds; standing architecture in the CLAUDE.md S3-F fold):**
  - **SR-1 (scope):** S3-F ABSORBS the cable↔line patch follow-on (OQ-R7-2 discharged) — the Link Cable column IS that family's surface.
  - **STRUCTURAL FINDING OF RECORD:** v1 has NO cable-side patch fields — a cable patched/branched to a line is held there entirely by clone-token enrollment (609 enrolls the branch cable's fields in the line's groups: Name↔Description, near/far device/connection, mult-name, line-number, color). **S3F-R1 (mechanism ruling):** v2 = the `cable_details` FK pair (`source/destination_patch_line_id/_end` → `cable_mult_lines`, ON DELETE SET NULL) + the R7 propagation engine; NO link groups minted (R7-6 and S3E-R6 stand). Ruled mechanism divergence, outcome parity. The Link Cable column = a reverse lookup over the FKs; many cables per line side legal (branch).
  - **S3F-R2:** script 665 (cable-form branch — Clone + wipe Group/Bundle/Length/Box + wild-line default Category/Method + end shuffle) REGISTERED, DEFERRED to its own follow-on with the wild-line defaults surface.
  - **S3F-R3 (as AMENDED):** empty cell = 📎 (branch-create); populated = **➡ ALONE** (R3-AMEND per s3f_01 — the "LINK badge" half resolved to B7's field-level badges, a different idiom) firing seamLanding onto the branch cable (selected + scrolled + Name focused; first-in-sort-order among multiple; v1 pedigree = script 41's link-token walk).
  - **S3F-R4:** `projects.branch_cable_type` (text, nullable; migration `s3f_branch_cable_type_default`, Arch-applied + verified) surfaced as an autocomplete "Branch Cable Type" field on the Cable Defaults panel; drives the 📎 model (empty → empty model, no dialog — v1 Default Ends behavior).
  - **S3F-R5:** line delete → cable FK nulls by schema + patched-end TEXT sweep on BOTH delete paths; cable delete owes the line nothing (it holds no FK; back-written text stands). Manual unpatch = the silent-unpatch shape (text-clear IS the unpatch; no invented control).
  - **S3F-R6 (the coupling map, as AMENDED):** couplings born at patch time, engine-carried, Update-Patches-gated: **(1)** line description ↔ cable NAME (the 616 rename inheritance, both directions — 421–424 prove the reverse) · **(2) FORWARD ONLY:** mult rename (the mult's OWN name field) → branch cables' near-end device text · **(3) FORWARD ONLY:** line renumber (the line's OWN number field) → branch cables' near-end connection (R7 dot convention) · **(4)+(5)** line chosen-end device/connection ↔ cable far-end device/connection, BOTH directions. **R6-AMEND-1 (Daniel-confirmed; CC's OQ recommendation adopted):** the REVERSE legs of 2/3 are STRUCK — a branch cable's near device/connection edit means CP-A patch RE-RESOLUTION (fresh/re-patch/steal/silent-unpatch), not rename/renumber; one gesture cannot mean both, and the referent's own field serves the rename. **R6-AMEND-2:** couplings 4/5 wired with RESOLUTION-WINS precedence — resolution runs first; only resolution-NO-OP ids get 4/5 propagation; never Make Patch AND Update Patches stacked on one edit.
  - **S3F-R7 (Daniel's colour rule, as AMENDED):** a patched/branch cable's colour links to the LINE's own colour if set, else the MULT's colour — evaluated at PROPAGATION TIME (dynamic; a deliberate divergence from both 681/683's no-colour-coupling and 609's frozen-at-enrollment choice). **R7-AMEND-1 (Daniel ruling, reversing CP-B's "colour never flows cable→line"):** a colour edit on a patched non-broken cable raises the B7 Update/Break/Cancel gate anatomy in colour copy — line-coupled → default UPDATE (the line's colour follows, then fans; Break/Cancel offered); mult-coupled → default BREAK (Update/Cancel offered; **Update repaints the MULT itself** — Daniel's registered ruling upheld over CC's uniform-line reading — then fans to non-broken cables on colourless lines, NO line write). Break persists via **`cable_details.patch_color_break`** (boolean NOT NULL default false; migration `s3f_patch_color_break` + the `v_cable_list` recreate w/ `security_invoker` explicitly re-specified and the column appended LAST — the only position CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW permits; Arch-applied + verified); broken = colour excluded from the coupling BOTH directions; any fresh patch/re-point RESETS the flag. **The mult-coupled Update leg ships known-defective in the browser = punch (www).** The dialog copy = CC's proposal, Daniel-approved (line body as written; mult body names the mult-repaint effect).

Arch source-grounded the design in the live file (sub-band is display-only at `EquipmentListClient.tsx:3122`; category sort collapses categories to the `'_'` bucket so the sub-band never renders there — the unit is purely additive). **No declared baseline expected**; the July-13 band amendment already gave the sub-band the same `.category-header` class as the top-level band.
- **S4-3 DESIGN GATE COMPLETE (Daniel, Aug 11)** — the full P2b decision block adjudicated ahead of the overnight run, so overnight CC never waits on a decision.
  - **Batch CONFIRMED as proposed: P1 · P2 · P4 · P5 · P6 · P7 · P8 · P9 · P10 · P11 · P13 · P14.** Texts of record in `handoffs/parallel/260811-0156_p2b_decision_block.md`; all evidence-cited, none costs schema.
  - **P3 RULED — box rename ships silently with a passive toast** ("Renamed for N items"). v1's *"Update Members?"* dialog **dies**: it exists in v1 only because membership is free-text and the cascade is a six-table Replace; under v2 FKs the rename is intrinsic and lossless, so the dialog would ask permission for an operation that no longer happens. Registered as a deliberate v1 deviation.
  - **P12 / M0-WIN RULED — the "0" convention becomes an explicit state on a PROTECTED SYSTEM ROW.** Strings: method **`N/A - Labels Only`** · box **`N/A - No Box Needed`** (the `N/A - <reason>` family — one idiom, field-specific tails because what is excluded differs). Renders at the **bottom of every relevant pulldown, below a divider, italic** (the gate-amendment-J sentinel idiom); method badge **grey + italic**, not a coloured pill; the N/A band **sorts last**. **`0` survives as an INPUT and SEARCH alias only, never a stored value** — typed it commits the state, searched it returns those items, and the v1 importer maps incoming `"0"` strings onto it, **through ONE shared predicate** consumed by the commit, find, and import paths. Daniel's design contribution of record: the pulldown placement and the interchangeability of `0` with the label for old-school users. Arch's shape contribution: the **protected** system row (not renamable, not deletable, seeded per project) — v1's magic record with guardrails, so a user cannot break the convention for a whole show. **Windowing ruled:** method-N/A items are **VISIBLE and editable on the on-screen equipment list** (banded last) and **EXCLUDED from the printed/exported equipment list, cable checklist, tail checklist, and Bundle Sheets**; box-N/A items are excluded from Box Contents output and do **not** trip the P5 unboxed check. This is the PORT-READY leg-2 criterion — **M0-WIN closes when built and gated**.
  - **s3g_census §7 Q1 CLOSED (Daniel, Aug 11):** the Method-0 exclusion **extends to Bundle Sheets** — three sites, matching what v1's `Layout-Bundle Sheets` actually does, rather than the two named in the 2026-07-21 rule of record.
  - **OQ1 RULED — "Show boxes within boxes" works under EVERY sort**, and the control is **greyed out** where it genuinely cannot apply. v1's `scripts/789` guards it to by-truck only while Box-cat §5.3 documents it as unconditional: the intent was general, the implementation partial.
  - **OQ2 RULED BY NEW EVIDENCE — R-51 CORRECTED** (see Corrections). Daniel supplied the first box output PDF in the corpus.
  - **OQ3 RULED — Box Types Volume is STORED and manually entered.** Daniel's reason of record: **volume is the box's INTERIOR capacity; W/D/H are EXTERIOR dimensions** — different measurements, so deriving one from the other is a category error, not a simplification. Consequence registered: nothing computes with W/D/H, so they stay **display-only text** and v1's fractional entry (`14 1/2`) survives. Both catalogs corrected (see Corrections).
  - **OQ4 RULED — ship the per-project Box Defaults panel**, seeded with v1's template constants: **5 big / 0 small labels**, category `11.0 Racks and Boxes`, method/group/header blank, Set-Remaining off. Storage follows the house precedent (`box_default_*` columns on `projects`, alongside the existing `equip_default_*` / `cable_default_*` / `tail_default_*` / `bundle_default_*`) — no new table.
  - **OQ5 RULED — Warn/Over volume thresholds are HARD-CODED at 23 / 27**, not per-project; they drop off the v1 Box Defaults panel (two fields removed, deliberately). Daniel's reason of record: percent-full measures nominal material volume against interior capacity, so a well-packed box reads ~23% once air and connector bodies are accounted for, and past 27% it is too cramped to work in. **Disclosed asymmetry:** `projects.bundle_warn_area_in2` / `bundle_over_area_in2` remain per-project on the bundle side; flagged, no action.
  - **OQ6 RULED — nesting cycles forbidden at write time, depth uncapped (R-50 stands).** UX on an attempted loop: **inline message beside the field, entry reverts, field stays open, no modal.** Database trigger is the backstop; the client message is the UX. *(Arch note of record: the first framing of this question was jargon-heavy and Daniel could not act on it — re-asked in plain language per the v28/v29 plain-language rule, which is working as a defect signal, not a favour.)*

### Aug-11 corrections (the S4-3 design gate session)

- **R-51 CORRECTED by new evidence.** As registered, R-51 said nested boxes carry an "Inside ⟨parent number⟩ ⟨parent name⟩" annotation in the by-truck Box List's **Notes** column. Daniel supplied the first box output PDF in the corpus (landed as `docs/reference/evidence/print/parity/v1_elephantshoes_boxlist_bytruck.pdf`) and it shows otherwise: the **Notes column is EMPTY** under by-truck — the `Combined` calc's suppression of both annotations is correct — and nested boxes instead render as **indented, italic, grey child rows in the NAME column, prefixed `Inside:`, carrying their OWN number and name**, immediately beneath their parent, which is never named because the parent-first sort guarantees it is the row above. The DDR extraction had predicted exactly this and could not resolve it ("five Text objects conditionally formatted on `nested AND by-truck` — consistent with a hide/indent treatment"). P2b's OQ2 is closed by artifact rather than by recall. **Also confirmed from the same PDF:** the column set `# · Name · Type · Location · Size (WxDxH) · Weight · Notes`; **decimal box numbers** (`8.5` slotting a parent between 8 and 9); unnumbered boxes sorting first as their own block; and **blank-beats-zero** rendering (no weight prints empty, never `0 lbs`). Partially closes digest gap **G2**.
- **Box-cat §3 and DDR-cat §1.7 CORRECTED.** Both state that Box Types' Volume "computes from W×D×H". **Both are wrong.** The DDR extraction is right — Volume is a plain editable Number, no extracted script computes it, and it travels as its own column in the 776/777 interchange. Daniel's ruling supplies the *reason* the catalogs could not: volume is INTERIOR capacity, W/D/H are EXTERIOR dimensions. The evidentiary hierarchy (DDR over screenshot-leg) resolved this correctly on the mechanics; the catalogs' error was semantic.
- **Arch self-correction — S4-3 schema block REV A withdrawn and replaced by REV B, same session.** REV A proposed adding `box_details.starred` and stated that "nothing in this block is required for the overnight run's first legs." Both were wrong, caught by reading `src/types/database.generated.ts` directly rather than trusting the P2b digest's NOTES: `equipment_items.starred` already exists and boxes inherit it, and the missing `box_details` field set **blocks the run's first leg**. Corrected before Daniel carried the file to the claude.ai seat. Standing lesson restated: the reference corpus describes v1; only the generated types describe v2.
- **Arch process correction — OQ6 asked badly.** The first framing ("forbid cycles, depth uncapped — the UX-on-cycle question") drew *"I don't understand the question"*. Re-asked in plain language against Daniel's own data (FOH Racks / FOH CPU Rack, from the PDF he had just supplied) and answered immediately. Logged per the v28 rule that treats an "explain plainly" response as a defect signal in the question, not a favour to the reader.

### Migrations 45–48 (Arch-applied, Trigger B) — the migration ledger moves 44 → 48

Each ran the full runbook: live introspection → proof-run with forced rollback → residue check → apply → post-apply verification.

- **45 · `s4_3_p12_box_side_to_flag_remove_na_box_rows`** — the P12 box side becomes a FLAG. The 21 seeded `N/A - No Box Needed` spine rows deleted; `box_details.is_system` dropped; `guard_system_box` dropped; **`equipment_items.no_box_needed`** added. `equipment_items` 1945 → 1924, `type='box'` 24 → 3 (`Moose`/`Salmon`/`Tryout`, real user boxes untouched). Method side unchanged — 21 system rows, `guard_system_method`, and the `seed_na_rows` trigger all intact, the trigger now seeding the method only. Probed: a new project gets 1 N/A method and **0** box rows.
- **46 · `s4_3_repoint_zero_alias_methods_to_na_labels_only`** — the six `(combo tails)` items on methods literally named `0` (JOY ×4, Vape! ×2) repointed to their project's `N/A - Labels Only`. **Deliberately not a deletion:** the emptied `0` rows now surface in each project's GCM Delete Unused, where Daniel removes them through the product. Before-state recorded in the migration comment for reversibility.
- **47 · `s4_3_projects_box_volume_thresholds`** — `projects.box_warn_volume` (23) + `box_over_volume` (27). Migration 43 had built six of the v1 Box Defaults panel's **eight** fields and omitted both Volume thresholds.
- **48 · `s4_3_seed_visual_gate_fixture_boxes`** — Visual Gate Fixture only: 4 boxes (typed/untyped/nested/trucked) + 3 `box_types` marked `notes='fixture'`. Scope proven post-apply: generic still 5, cable 1, categories 2, methods 4 — so frames 02/03/05/08 could not move.

### Rulings of record (Daniel)

- **OQ-F — a box prints under whatever category it carries.** Verbatim: *"a box is a thing, so it displays under whatever category it has in the box list. I frequently call this category 11, but a user could call it any category."* **Never key logic to a category name.** Corroborated independently in code: `lib/queries/equipment.ts` — *"the printed list is the shop order — v1 functional truth."* The print path's unfiltered read is correct and was never the defect.
- **The P12 asymmetry is correct.** `methods` is a vocabulary table and can carry a "none of the above"; `equipment_items` is a things table and cannot carry a non-thing. Arch's earlier symmetry argument was the wrong abstraction, owned.
- **The `0`-alias items are the convention's first real members**, not junk — they generate labels but not gear, which is what `N/A - Labels Only` means.
- **Fixture box seeding approved** as a declared event; frame 07 refreshed accordingly, and now guards box-print behaviour.
- **REVERT of the equipment-list print layout to its Aug 4 state** (`40aa945`), applied at `9c3932f`. Scope verified file-by-file before applying: two files, one line, one baseline event; the revision-notes fix, legs 0–1, the types regen and the nine cable/tail print surfaces all confirmed outside the blast radius.

### Arch rulings (delegated, cited)

OQ-E baseline events (scoped, after treatment) · OQ-G `box_types.dimensions` = dead weight · OQ-H protected row stays fully read-only · OQ-I exclude the N/A row from the "managed methods" count (deferred, never built) · OQ-J find/import wiring rides the legs that touch them · OQ-K lint · OQ-Q `.eqp-r` → 10pt (deferred, never built) · OQ-R wrap divisor = 85 chars, measured · OQ-S `METHOD_HDR_H` stays derived, registered as fixture debt · **OQ-T the revision highlight covers the model row ONLY, unconditionally** · OQ-U the 1px left, deliberately.

### CORRECTIONS — Arch (all owned)

1. **A standing-rule violation instructed.** Arch's commit instruction said "push to `main` and report the deployment hash." **CLAUDE.md WF-1a: "push is always Daniel's paste."** Arch had no authority to direct a push; CC's return reports one. Owned in full — the instruction was wrong regardless of outcome, and the rule stands unamended.
2. **The migration-44 seed defect.** The P12 box row printed as a phantom equipment line on **all 21 projects**, including Matchbook Festival, a live show. Arch verified the on-screen list filtered `type='generic'` and never checked the print path. Root cause: a non-thing inserted into a things table, not a print bug.
3. **HOTFIX-1 §3 specified the wrong fix** — row-level, uneditable note display — driven by an unmeasured claim that per-item attribution was "mathematically unrecoverable." It was true of **11 of 1,366 items (0.8%)**. Corrected in HOTFIX-1a after measuring.
4. **The highlight "exception" was ruled from inference.** Arch told CC struck-through removed rows carry the fill through their notes. Pixel measurement across every highlighted block on three v1 pages: **zero note words inside any green fill**. Retracted.
5. **The note-column width was put to Daniel as a design question** when v1's own geometry already answered it — notes stop at x=445, exactly where `From` begins. Measurement should have preceded the question.
6. **Two column-geometry defects were diagnosed and then left as open questions rather than ruled**, so they went unbuilt for two passes while type was corrected around them. This is the sequencing error behind Daniel's *"it feels like we're pushing the problem around."*
7. **A stale handoff §4** listed the notes-to-print bug as open after CC had built it; files crossed.

### NOT corrections — CC work of record

CC's halt on the seed drift (refusing to build six legs on a red gate) · distinguishing the `04-library` failure from the registered dark-only flake by near-identical both-theme pixel counts · catching a **clipped document** by reading a recaptured frame where no threshold would have fired · the `item.notes === undefined` key-absence guard (a falsy guard would have silently overwritten deliberate blanks forever) · the React unitless-`lineHeight` catch (`LEAD_NOTE = 16` → a 213px line box), found by probe, not by reading CSS · **refusing to build the highlight rule without a v1 PDF in the repo**, which is what forced the measurement that corrected Arch · two sed disclosures, one self-reported as a defect and undefended · two self-inflicted JSX syntax errors disclosed rather than tidied.

### THE PRINT-PARITY CYCLE — closed, reverted, and fully documented

Five hotfix passes (HOTFIX-1/1a through HOTFIX-5) corrected type scale, leading, page box and column geometry in turn; the result read worse than the starting point and Daniel ruled the revert. **Everything measured survives** in `docs/reference/Minotaur_v1_Print_Spec_Measured.md` (builder-facing) and the project doc `Minotaur_Print_Parity_Findings_2026-08-11.md` (narrative + process).

**The three findings worth carrying:**

1. **The Aug-4 document was a uniform ~75% scale of v1** — internally consistent, which is why it read correctly. **v2's leading ratio has never matched v1's** (~1.40–1.62 against ~1.20–1.26). Correcting type without leading took a 75%-sized error and made it full-size. **Type and leading are one correction.**
2. **The visual gate was structurally blind to the surface it was meant to guard.** AMEND-3 C4 confined the point-true sizes to print media expressly *"so the frame-07 photograph is byte-unchanged"* — so no gate run could ever see the printed type scale, and a 25%-too-small body reached a live show's paperwork.
3. **Dev-server exports are inadmissible as parity evidence.** Stale chunks produced **three** misleading measurements in one day, each triggering a real diagnosis of a non-existent problem. **New standing rule:** print-parity judgements come from `rm -rf .next && npm run build && npm start` or a deployment only, and every print report states its build provenance. This joins the Print doctrines in CLAUDE.md.

- **PRINT-REDO** — the eventual re-attempt at equipment-list print parity. Opens only on Daniel's word, with a single agreed target ruled first, a measurement harness built before any layout change, and both cascades made to agree so frame 07 can guard the surface. Sprint-4 close at the earliest.
- **CLAUDE-FIX** — CLAUDE.md's fixture pristine-state is stale twice over (the N/A method row, then the four seeded boxes + three box_types). Text proposed by CC in `from-cc/260811-1250`; Daniel confirms and commits.

- **UNALLOC** — unallocate does not appear to return a cable (and perhaps tails) to a generic item. The cable path exists and flips `type→'generic'` (`CableListClient.tsx:2824`/`:2910`, `cableWrites.ts:14`); suspects are the tail surface having no equivalent, or a cache-invalidation gap. Needs a reproduction from Daniel.
- **LINT** — amended: there is **no ESLint config anywhere in the repo**. Next 16 removed `next lint`, which was the only thing supplying one. Lint has never run in v2; standing it up is config authoring, not a fix.
- **PARITY-PAIR** — every v1↔v2 comparison this cycle pitted a v1 *revision* list against a v2 *preliminary* list, which are different layouts. **A v1 preliminary list and a v2 revision-mode output from early August are both missing** and would make the next attempt tractable.

## AUGUST 12–13, 2026 — S4-3 LEG 2 CLOSED: THE ⊕ CREATE DIALOG, THREE AMEND ROUNDS, FOUR BROWSER GATES (append block)

### Rulings of record

- **R-A (Daniel, Aug 12) — Enter HOLDS an unmatched box-type name; the ⊕ opens a create dialog.** Silently reverting the operator's typing is wrong. Pedigree: v1 ("v1 holds it as unmade until you hit the plus button") plus v2's own add-a-cable-to-a-nonexistent-bundle idiom (`UpdateBundleDialog`, create mode; `CableListClient.tsx:1677` gates before create; `:1890` Cancel leaves nothing). The dialog collects Name · Width · Depth · Height · Weight · Internal Volume.
- **BOX-VOL-R1 WITHDRAWN as a duplicate ID — the ruling is OQ3 (Aug 11).** Arch minted a new ID for a rule that already had one. Daniel restated OQ3's reasoning unprompted and identically eleven days later: internal volume is entered manually, never computed from outside dimensions, because walls, foam and casters make interior capacity a different measurement from exterior size. The build instruction was unchanged and correct; only the citation was wrong.
- **R-B (Daniel) — Change All REFUSALS become modal; CONFIRMATIONS stay passive.** P3's green rename toast is untouched. Refusing an action the operator asked for should stop them; confirming one they completed should not. `ConfirmDialog` gained an opt-in `singleAction`.
- **R-C (Daniel) — the footer inspector loses its four duplicate status checkboxes and the computed weight+cost readout.** `Add'l Weight` / `Actual Weight` / `Add'l Cost` are KEPT and remain editable: they are the only entry point for those values anywhere in the app. Arch flagged the distinction before building; Daniel confirmed the boundary.
- **OQ2 (Daniel) — a held box-type draft REVERTS on leaving the ROW.** It survives Tab onto the ⊕, the dialog opening, and Cancel. Reason of record: an unfocused cell showing a value the database does not have, on a row the operator has walked away from, reads as SAVED — a lie on screen in a dark theatre, and the yellow untyped fill is not enough signal alone. R-A protects typing from the gesture just made; it does not make the draft durable state.
- **The same-row idiom: REVERT via A2's existing path (CC's call, Arch-accepted).** CC built a same-row HOLD first and the browser smoke proved it unreachable — once focus is back in the field the cell is an ordinary autocomplete, and moving within the row is an outside-click commit, which for unmatched text is A2's ruled `revert`. Buying the hold meant forking A2's semantics for one cell to keep a value on screen the database lacks, which is what OQ2's own reason argues against.
- **OQ4 (Daniel) — the create dialog carries ONE line of body copy:** *Add "⟨name⟩" to this project's Box Types. Size and volume are optional.* No v1 copy exists to cite — v1's ⊕ has no dialog at all (Box Catalog §2.4.1) — so this is a v2 invention off the bundle idiom, pedigree (a). **Deliberately omits "you can fill them in later"**: the Box Types admin is leg 4 and does not exist yet; a test now fails if anyone adds the clause before it ships. Blank name degrades to *Add a type to this project's Box Types.*
- **R-D (Daniel, Aug 13) — Tab out of the Type cell OPENS the create dialog** rather than landing focus on the ⊕. One gesture where the build had two, and safe for the same reason capture-Enter is: the dialog gates the create, so Tab raises something confirmable and never writes. Accepted cost, stated at ruling time: Tab is the app's "move on" key, so Tabbing out of a cell holding unmatched text now raises a modal. Shift+Tab stays ordinary.
- **The ⊕ condition is PRESENCE, not provenance (Arch, Aug 13).** The ⊕ hides whenever an option is currently highlighted in the open dropdown, **however it got highlighted**. A highlighted row is a promise that Enter takes it; the ⊕ must not contradict a promise the screen is making. Corollary ruled with it: a hover-set highlight clears when the pointer leaves the list, a keyboard-set one survives — otherwise a stale hover strands the ⊕ invisible with no keyboard route to creation.
- **Declared baseline event GRANTED for frames 09/10/11/12 × both themes (8 frames).** Arch's §6 predicted "frame 10 only"; the footer panel is `always visible` by construction (CP-B1) and is therefore in-frame in all four box captures. CC measured per-frame, proved every changed pixel fell inside one 213×62 box in the panel's column 4, and re-baselined NOTHING on its own authority.
- **KNOW-HYG (Arch proposal, Daniel BANKED for Arch Prompt v30).** Close hygiene for claude.ai project knowledge: retire a superseded governing doc in the same step that installs its replacement; never manually upload a document the GitHub connector already syncs. Not cut as its own version — a natural v30 is already forming (COWORK-REV's ruling, COWORK-PORT, WF1A-SUNSET, seat consolidation). Operating from the docket meanwhile.
- **The Sed Disclosure Rule is widened to the RISK CLASS, not the tool name** (CLAUDE.md, Aug 13): it governs any repo-file modification made through the shell, including a `python3`/`node` heredoc, because all of them bypass the PostToolUse frozen-check hook.

### Corrections of record

- **FOUR Arch corrections this session, and they are one pattern: specifying or asserting against a REMEMBERED or INFERRED surface instead of a MEASURED one.**
  1. The AMEND-2 re-gate script opened the B6 test with "click into Header" — Header is display-only on `BoxRow`, so the stale-arm half of the test could not run, and B6's real root cause sat unverified until a re-test.
  2. AMEND-3 §6 predicted "frame 10 only" without checking that the footer panel is always visible; eight frames moved.
  3. AMEND-3 §6 required the five source-pinned gate tests to survive *while also* ordering the keyboard route restored — four of them pinned its ABSENCE. A self-contradictory instruction; CC re-pointed them and flagged it rather than fixing it silently, and the replacements are a stronger gate than the block they replaced.
  4. Arch told Daniel the dialog's blank-name path was dangerous ("OK will clear the box's type") by reasoning from `planBoxTypeCommit` without checking that `planNewBoxType` guards first and refuses. Daniel's screenshot disproved it.
  **This is the same failure the Aug-11 entry already recorded** ("never design against a remembered schema — only the generated types describe v2") reappearing in the UI, the harness and the test layers. **Standing correction: read the surface before writing the instruction that constrains it.** All four were caught downstream — twice by CC, twice by Daniel — which is the system working, but each cost a round.
- **CAT-VOL-FIX — corrected in the corpus, not just in this Ledger.** `docs/reference/Minotaur_v1_Box_Behavioral_Catalog.md` §3 and `v1-cable-box-catalog/Minotaur_v1_Cable_Box_Catalog.md` both still claimed Box Types' Volume "computes from W×D×H" — the exact claim this Ledger's Aug-11 block had already recorded as wrong. Both now carry the correction inline with its date and ruling.

### THE PROPAGATION THEME — a correction that lands only in the Ledger is INERT

Four instances surfaced in one session: the volume rule (Ledger corrected, catalogs did not), CLAUDE.md's handoff protocol (v29 retired the Desktop path, CLAUDE.md did not), CLAUDE.md's run fence (stale by two days), and the AMEND-2 gate tests (pinning a shape a later ruling superseded). **The Ledger and Roadmap capture rulings; CLAUDE.md and the reference catalogs are what CC actually READS.** Registered as the second Arch Prompt v30 candidate alongside KNOW-HYG: when a ruling corrects a prior statement, the close names every document carrying the old text and updates them in the same package — a propagation check, not a memory test.

### Work of record — CC

- **The D1 root cause underneath Arch's root cause.** Arch identified that `showTypePlus` ignored a navigated highlight. CC then found the deeper defect at Daniel's "works sometimes": the flag recorded **how** the highlight was set, not **whether** one existed — `onMouseEnter` set the highlight and nothing else, so the create gate stayed open through the mouse door while every probe drove it by keyboard. The fix derives presence from render state and announces it from ONE effect, "so no code path can set a highlight and forget to announce it, which is the exact failure mode D4 was."
- **Three defects found by CC's own browser probe before reaching Daniel:** the dialog's Cancel reading as an outside-click and wiping the held draft (R-A defeated); arrow-selection leaving the cell showing the partial; and the same-row hold being unreachable.
- **Refusing to re-baseline on its own authority**, and refusing to adopt frame 10 alone because a knowingly inconsistent baseline set is worse than none.
- **Proving the 02/03 dark byte-drift with controls** (frame 13 negative, 09-light positive) and handing the restore line to Daniel per the standing rider rather than running it.
- **Disclosing the `python3` heredoc unprompted**, on the reasoning that a bash write bypasses the frozen hook even though the rule as written named only stream editors — with a single-match assertion per pattern and the frozen gate run immediately after. Logged for WF1A-SUNSET as evidence the RULE was underspecified, **explicitly not as a discipline strike**; the prior two `perl -pi` datapoints were the opposite behaviour.
- **Reading the gate against the standing rule rather than the tool's verdict** when eight frames sat nonzero under the threshold.

- **Flake (3) amendment DISCHARGED into CLAUDE.md** — a long-lived dev server is a trigger for the fail-hard; the remedy is restarting it, not re-running against it.
- **NEW — threshold vs declared event.** The AMEND-3 gate printed `PASSED` with eight nonzero frames because all sat under the 0.1000% threshold (top 0.0906%). Only the any-nonzero-is-a-finding rule caught it; **a real regression up to 0.0999% would print PASSED.** The rule is doing work the tool should do. On HARNESS.

The body band at `top=206` draws two object classes — editable `FieldObj` (Box Name 14 · Box Type 217 · **Box Header 398 · Box Number 498 · Truck Manual 577 · Box Stack 676 · Box Location 786**, plus the four checkbox fields Remaining 922 · HistoryIDOpen 957 "Star" · Drawn 1005 · Packed 1059) and display-only merge `Text` (`<<Percent Full>>%` 850 · `<<Cost>>` 963 · `<<Weight>>` 1077 · `(in <<Box in Box>>)` 582). v1's footer band (`top=240–283`) contains **none** of the five. **The row is their parity home, not the panel.** Daniel confirmed all five in one unit rather than splitting Stack out.

**TAB-HATCH — RULED: no dedicated hatch (Daniel, Aug-13 browser gate).** R-D stands unchanged (Tab with a held box-type draft raises the create dialog). Escape-then-Tab is the hatch, and with the five cells live, Tab from the Type cell now reaches Header rather than stranding. Gated in practice, not reasoned.

**Nested-row treatment — RULED AS BUILT (Daniel, Aug-13 browser gate):** *"this behavior is good — leave as is."* On a nested row the Truck cell is not editable (the `(in ⟨parent⟩)` text stays, R-50) and is skipped in the tab order; Stack stays editable and in the order.

**The nested-row hop — RULED (Arch, Aug 13).** A ⌥⇧↑/↓ hop whose desired column does not exist on the landing row **moves the record, focuses nothing, and remembers the column**, so the next hop resumes it. Falling forward to Stack or back to Header would silently move an operator walking the Truck column into a different field, where the next thing typed lands wrong; refusing the hop strands them. Written against a `fieldExists` predicate rather than against Truck, so the next conditional cell inherits it. **Superseded in part the same evening by PANEL-NAV** for the panel-field case (see below).

**Footer Enter — RULED (b), commits and blurs (Arch, Aug 13), then DEFERRED-SUPERSEDED.** Arch's §7 instruction pointed the wrong way against its own citation; CC measured and stopped rather than building it. `CableInspectorPanel.tsx:141-142` and the box panel's own `DetailText:141` are byte-for-byte the same rule — commit and blur — so aligning to "commit and stay" would have changed the text cells against their cited artifact. Built as (b): all four panel cell kinds now follow one Enter rule, textareas exempt. **Daniel then ruled at the re-gate that the eventual rule should be advance-to-next-record (PANEL-NAV, post-private-beta).** Noted of record: the single rule was the precondition for changing the rule.

**TYPE-HOLD — MILESTONE TRIGGER B halted, three rulings banked, build deferred (Daniel, Aug 13).** Daniel **reversed OQ2**: an unmade box-type name should stay in the cell, yellow, with the ⊕ up, rather than discarding on row exit — and stored, the way v1 stores it. v1 can because Box Type is a text field with a value list (the yellow is the conditional format `Box List::Box Type ≠ Box Types::Box Type`); v2's is a foreign key, so this needs a column. Arch issued a **[MILESTONE HALT]**; Daniel **authorized a SEAT-MIG exception** for the build and mapped it to Sprint-4 close / private-beta prep, leaving current behaviour in place. Rulings: **TH-R1** healing is automatic — a box adopts the real type the moment one with that name exists, no touch, no prompt. **TH-R2** the name prints regardless of whether it is a defined type. **TH-R3** Change All writes the name and the flag travels with it; creating the type heals every box at once, TH-R1 doing the work — making Change All plus one ⊕ a legitimate bulk workflow.

**Change All gains `stack`** per **CA-BOX** (Name and In Box remain the only exclusions; everything else bulk-applies per R-8). Verified from all five row cells at Daniel's gate, and the Name cell still returns the R-B refusal modal.

(4) §9 and §12 cited two CLAUDE.md rules that did not yet exist at HEAD — forward-dated citations leaning entirely on a §0 precondition to catch them. All four are the standing Aug-13 correction firing again: **read the surface before writing the instruction that constrains it**; (2) adds its sibling, **a declared event's scope is measured, not reasoned**. Both are banked for Arch Prompt v30.

**A governing-doc delivery defect, and its fix.** The amended CLAUDE.md from the Aug-12–13 close went out as a download, was never saved, and blocked leg 3 at its §0 precondition the next morning — CC halted with the run fence still reading `S4-AUTO (S4-2) OPEN at 1426e03`, two units stale. Root cause: v29 §Seats makes the repo root read-only to the Cowork seat, so a repo-root file can only leave as a download, the one delivery form with no trace on disk. Fix of record, banked for v30: **every repo-root deliverable also gets a `Minotaur-Cowork/drafts/` copy.** The regenerated file was produced by editing the staged repo copy rather than retyping it — 82,217 → 86,112 bytes, five diff hunks at exactly five intended locations, 36 removed lines accounted for line by line.

### Aug 14 rulings + closes, part 2 (the HARNESS-H2 / PORT-COV session — Cowork principal seat, opened after the leg-3 close installed)

- **HARNESS H2/H3 SCHEDULING RULED (Daniel, Aug 14): H2 as its own unit, ahead of leg 4.** Arch's recommendation taken over both stated alternatives (H2+H3 bundled; leg 4 first under the hand discipline), on the grounds that legs 4–6 each write a declaration and shipping without scoped writes would leave the pathology H2 exists to remove live for three legs. **H3's build is NOT scheduled**; its step-1 positional control (two gate runs, zero code) rides leg 4's gate runs, which produce the datapoint anyway.
- **HARNESS-H2 CLOSED at `ddcf929`** (Aug 14, 4 files, +1075/−103, one CC session + one amend round, WF-1a held, push refs-verified `254ec1c..ddcf929`, production READY at `ddcf929` exit 0). `--update-baselines` now refuses without `--declare <path>`, measures the moved set itself, hard-fails on any disagreement writing **nothing**, and on agreement writes **only** the declared frames, printing both lists so the run's output is the record. The comparator is a pure module (`scripts/lib/declaredEvent.mjs`, `classifyFrames`) with nine numbered rules, `toWrite` empty whenever `ok` is false; 34 tests. `--gate --emit-declaration <path>` emits a candidate from what the gate measured (bbox padded 2px/side, `maxPixels` = measured × 1.1 + 8, every `reason` blank on purpose and refused if left blank). **`git checkout --` has left the baseline workflow, and the same-run restore trap has nothing left to revert** — the Aug-12 CLAUDE.md rider retires with the mechanism it guarded, not with better documentation. Suite 1514/101 → **1548/102**; frozen 3/3 vs `be0769de`; visual 26/26 @ 0.0000%; `screenshots/baseline/` byte-identical after four capture runs across the unit.
- **H2 judges the moved set at the gate's EXISTING threshold, deliberately** — zero tolerance would make the 02/03 drift hard-fail every declared event and put H2 behind H3/H4. Rules 3, 4 and 9 consume the zero-tolerance measurements. Tightening the moved-set definition stays H4's job, and H2 leaves it a clean seam: one constant plus the registry.
- **Rule 9 registered (Arch ruling, Aug 14, on CC's OQ1):** `declared region far larger than measured`. Rule 3's containment check had no upper bound, so a whole-frame bbox satisfied it for any movement anywhere — the check reading as satisfied while doing nothing, which CC found by using it in its own scratch declaration. Ruled as a NINTH numbered rule rather than an extension of rule 3 (different failure, different message), and as an **area ratio relative to the measurement** rather than an absolute cap — the abuse is orders of magnitude, the false-positive risk is a few percent of shrink, and an absolute cap would reject a legitimately whole-frame event. `area(declared) <= area(measured) × FACTOR + ADD`. **Rule 4 guards the flank**: rule 9 bounds the region, rule 4 bounds the pixel volume, and the pair is tight in a way neither is alone — which is why a generous ADD costs nothing.
- **DECLARE_BBOX_MAX_AREA_ADD shipped at 4000, not the ruled 400 — CC's argument accepted, and Arch's constant was wrong.** The handoff's own required sanity check ("the emit path stays legal") fails at 400: padding a THIN measurement 2px per side multiplies its area rather than nudging it — a 1596×1 full-width line emits at 1600×5 = 8000 px² against a ceiling of 5188, so the harness's own emitted declaration would have failed its own rule. CC swept every w×h to 1600×1000; worst overshoot 3212 px² at exactly that shape. FACTOR untouched at 3.0. Both worst-case shapes pinned by tests so the constant going stale is caught rather than discovered.
- **Two Arch corrections, both CC-caught — standing-correction instances EIGHT and NINE.** (8) The H2 handoff's §0 asserted `visual-capture.mjs` at **633 lines** where it is **632**; CC proved byte-identity to `254ec1c` with `git diff HEAD` and proceeded rather than halting on a proxy. The lesson is sharper than the miss: **a precondition must assert the property it cares about, not a proxy for it** — the intent was "this file has not moved," and a line count was a stand-in whose counting convention had not been checked. (9) §10 instructed "stage by explicit filename and stop," contradicting CLAUDE.md line 253 — **"No phrase, no stage, no commit."** CC declined to stage and flagged it as a handoff defect rather than following. Both are the Surface Rule's own species: text that was already in front of Arch.
- **§6's propagation list was short, and CC left the neighbours correctly.** The amendment named the run fence but not the two-units-stale paragraphs around it (the leg-2 opener with counts "1461/97", the "CURRENT UNIT IS S4-3 LEG 3 — B4 FIRST" block, the BOX-CELLS/layout-179 paragraph, B4's browser-gate checklist). CC left all of them under §6's own "not on the list is an OPEN QUESTION, not a judgment call" instruction, and AMEND-1 brought the whole section current. Arch's omission; the propagation check catching Arch rather than the reverse, third instance this week.
- **The sub-threshold drift is renamed of record: "02/03-dark byte-drift" is too narrow.** Four capture runs in one session landed on four different sets — 12-pair only (light 6 px Δ1 bbox 262,107–299,108; dark 6 px Δ2 bbox 597,108–723,108) · 02/03-light only (16 px) · none · 02/03 both themes (16 px, bbox 1582,18–1583,27). The 02/03 instances all sit at the topbar glyph and fit the parallel block's warm-up-boundary model; **the 12-pair is a second site the model never addressed**, at y≈107–108 and — the part worth carrying — **at different x ranges in light and dark for the same frame**, which is not a shared-element signature. H3's remit grows; the single-pill diagnosis is incomplete rather than wrong. Every one of the four went undeclared and was therefore never written: **"inert by construction" observed, not asserted.**
- **IMP-SPINE registered — a live data-loss path found by audit (Arch, Aug 14; NOT designed, rides leg 4).** The exporter filters `equipment_items` to `type = 'generic'` (`runExport.ts:238`); **the importer's current-state fetch does not** (`ImportV1Client.tsx:63–75`, `project_id` only). Mirror mode plans a delete for every current row the incoming sheet does not match (`diff.ts:133–142`), and cable/tail/box rows can never match — their v1 sheets are register-only. `import_apply_v1` deletes exactly those ids, scoped by `project_id` alone. Live exposure at audit: **41 cable · 14 tail · 13 box rows**, against 1,930 identity-map rows all carrying `entity_table='equipment_items'`. Cascade measured: `cable_details`, `tail_details`, `box_details`, `cable_mult_lines` are ON DELETE CASCADE off the spine row; `bundles.box_id`, `equipment_items.box_id`, `cable_details.source/destination/split_of/allocated_from`, `tail_details.parent_cable_id`, `box_details.within_box_id` are SET NULL, so surviving cables lose links. **The pre-apply "snapshot" does not cover it** — it is `buildRevisionSnapshot` over `v_equipment_list`, a printed-list artifact carrying no cable, tail, bundle or box detail. The dry-run does show the deletes, labelled "Equipment" and rendered by `model`, so a cable row appears unlabeled: the gate is visible but does not communicate the risk. Split OUT of PORT-COV of record — PORT-COV is coverage, this is a scoping defect on a destructive path.

- **PORT-COV rulings (Daniel, Aug 14 — all as recommended):** **(1)** identity entries partition by the detail table's physical name — `'cable_details'` · `'tail_details'` · `'box_details'` · `'bundles'` · `'cable_mult_lines'` — no schema change; a CHECK constraint later would be its own Trigger B, and the current-state fetch's hardcoded single label becomes per-partition queries. **(2)** non-UUID historyIDs (script 506's timestamp+random backfill) are treated as null and carried by the row hash; widening `v1_history_id` to text is declined — measured basis: zero non-UUID IDs in the JOY corpus, 12 null IDs in 1,957 rows. **(3)** **recompute** is blessed as a third import class of record alongside mirror and register-only, stated generally rather than for the two clone-links sheets alone. **(4)** **the round-trippable column set is script 567's own field map, per sheet** — see below. **(5)** Groups' `Notes` and `Start Date` are **deprecated** (0/18 populated in JOY; v1 does round-trip them by design, so this is a deliberate drop, not an oversight). **(6)** the equip export's `Box` column **emits the box's name** — RC-8's blank-emit deferral is amended, its stated reason having expired when `equipment_items.box_id` landed; evidence three ways (DDR: `Equipment Manually Added::Box` is Text/Normal, no calc · rel 80 joins the bare `Box Name`, not the `Box Number Name` composite · value list 24 writes names). Write-only until the boxes importer lands, and now carrying the EXPORT-ORDER constraint below. **(7)** the `RF Cities.xlsx` → `rf city list.xlsx` filename correction is taken with its propagation list (Mapping Spec §1 · `spec.ts:266` · the opener family).
- **Ruling 4 in its final form, and it came from v1 rather than from us.** Script `567_Import Show` maps every re-ingest **positionally** (`Source field N import to Table::Field`) and its map has deliberate gaps — the gaps are the derived columns. Cable imports `Box Manually Added`, `Group Manually Added`, `Length Manual`, `Packed Manual` and never their derived twins; Mult Lines imports only the six ` Manual` twins. **So the hash-input set is a list to read, not a principle to apply.** One precision of record: 567 imports **both** members of the three ` Original` pairs (`Flagged End`, `Split End`, `This is a Mult`), so ` Original` is NOT a calc-twin marker and is excluded from the rule — only ` Manual` and ` Manually Added` mark the stored twin.
- **v1 has no mirror semantics at all — stated of record because the identity design's justification depended on believing it did.** Every step of 567 is `Method: Add`. v1's round-trip is export-everything → import-into-a-blank-file: no update, no delete, no reconciliation, and **HistoryID is imported as data, never used as a match key**. Mirror mode and `import_identity_map` are v2 inventions that work *because* v1's HistoryIDs are stable, not because v1 uses them that way. The design stands; its borrowed precedent does not.
- **EXPORT-ORDER registered.** Because v1's re-ingest is positional, column ORDER is part of the contract. 567's boxes map runs to source field **38** where JOY's `boxes.xlsx` carries **36** columns — v1's own import expects a wider file than v1's own export writes (this also reconciles the digest's "38 of 45" against the measured 36). Any v2-emitted v1-compatible export must match the order 567 expects, not merely the names — a live constraint on ruling 6.
- **WIRE-TYPE registered.** `cable.xlsx` exports `Wire Type` and `Wire Type Quantity`; 567 maps field 60 → `Cable::Wire Type`; v2 has no column for either in `cable_details` or `cable_types`. Confirmed from three sides rather than inferred from absence.

Containers are settled by v1's own closing dialog: *"Photos weren't imported — sorry."*
